#### Method 1 — what it is in the chart
- Numbers (10):
  - RA to Ketu = **whole 180** (180.0000, off -0.0000; ALL DAY)
  - Sky to Ketu = **whole 180** (180.0000, off +0.0000; ALL DAY)
  - Dec to Antares ★ = **whole 3** (2.9987, off -0.0013; ALL DAY)
